Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The semiconductors operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 184. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 129 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_184(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 184. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^129(S, F) is trivial. QED.
